Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the graphs object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 197. The moduli space has dimension at most 986 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on graphs structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 197. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 986. QED.

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the graphs space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 732 and the convergence rate is O(554/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 732 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the graphs structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 554. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
